\Sigma\backslash[-1,1]
into
\Sigma\backslash([-1,1]\cup U_{1/n}(1)\cup U_{1/n}(-1))
and
(\Sigma\backslash[-1,1])\cap U_{1/n}(1)
and
(\Sigma\backslash[-1,1])\cap U_{1/n}(-1)
.

B.2
Some important norms

Proposition B.2
.

The functions
\mu
,
\tilde{\mu}
obey the following estimates:

\displaystyle\big|\big|\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n)}\big)\big|\big|_{L^{2}(\Sigma)}=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{1/2}\log^{2}n}\right),

(B.15)

\displaystyle\big|\big|\mu^{(n)}-\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big|\big|_{L^{2}(\Sigma)}=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{1/2}\log^{2}n}\right),

(B.16)

\displaystyle\big|\big|\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n)}\big)-\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}\big)\big|\big|_{L^{2}(\Sigma)}=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{3/2}\log^{2}n}\right),

(B.17)

\displaystyle\big|\big|\big(\mu^{(n)}-\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big)-\big(\mu^{(n+1)}-\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}\big)\big|\big|_{L^{2}(\Sigma)}=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{3/2}\log^{2}n}\right).

(B.18)

Proof.

Recall that
\delta>0
is fixed as
n\to\infty
and that
v_{\Sigma}(s)-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}(s)\equiv 0
for
s\in[-1,1]
and
U_{1/n}(s)
refers to the ball of radius
1/n
centered at
s=\pm 1
. We have that

\displaystyle\big|\big|\tilde{\mu}\left(v_{\Sigma}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}\right)\big|\big|_{L^{2}(\Sigma)}^{2}=\int_{\Sigma\backslash[-1,1]}\left|\tilde{\mu}\left(v_{\Sigma}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}\right)\right|^{2}|{\rm d}s|

\displaystyle=\left(\int_{(\Sigma\backslash[-1,1])\cap U_{1/n}(1)}\!+\int_{(\Sigma\backslash[-1,1])\cap U_{1/n}(-1)}\!+\int_{\Sigma\backslash([-1,1]\cup U_{1/n}(-1)\cup U_{1/n}(1))}\right)\!\left|\tilde{\mu}\left(v_{\Sigma}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}\right)\right|^{2}|{\rm d}s|

\displaystyle=I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}.

(B.19)

First we will bound

\displaystyle I_{3}=\int_{\Sigma\backslash([-1,1]\cup U_{1/n}(-1)\cup U_{1/n}(1))}\left|\tilde{\mu}\left(v_{\Sigma}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}\right)\right|^{2}|{\rm d}s|,

which will be done by first proving
L^{\infty}
bounds on
\tilde{\mu}_{2}
and
\phi^{-2n}
. From (
B.12
),

\displaystyle\sup_{z\in[1+1/n,1+\delta]}\left|\tilde{\mu}_{2}(z)\right|=\sup_{\frac{1}{n}\leqslant z-1\leqslant\delta}\big|R^{(n)}(z)E(z)(2\pi n)^{\sigma_{3}/2}\Psi_{2}(n^{2}f(z))\phi^{n}(z)\big|

\displaystyle\leqslant cn^{1/2}\sup_{\frac{1}{n}\leqslant z-1\leqslant\delta}\big|\Psi_{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\phi^{n}(z)\big|

\displaystyle\hphantom{\sup_{z \in[1+ 1/n,1+\delta]}\left|\tilde{\mu}_2(z) \right|}{}=cn^{1/2}\sup_{\frac{1}{n}\leqslant z-1\leqslant\delta}\big|\Psi_{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)e^{2(n^{2}f(z))^{1/2}}\big|

\displaystyle\hphantom{\sup_{z \in[1+ 1/n,1+\delta]}\left|\tilde{\mu}_2(z) \right|}{}=cn^{1/2}\sup_{O(n)\leqslant y\leqslant O(n^{2})}\left|\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}(y)e^{2y^{1/2}}\\
\Psi_{22}(y)e^{2y^{1/2}}\end{matrix}\right|,

where
y=n^{2}f(z)
. Substituting in the asymptotics of
\Psi
as in
(\ref{PsiAsymptotics})
, we see that

\displaystyle\sup_{z\in[1+1/n,1+\delta]}\left|\tilde{\mu}_{2}(z)\right|=O(n)

(B.20)

Similarly

\displaystyle\sup_{z\in(\Sigma\backslash[-1,1])\cap(U_{\delta}(-1)\backslash U_{1/n}(-1))}\left|\tilde{\mu}_{2}(z)\right|

\displaystyle\qquad{}\leqslant cn^{1/2}\sup_{z\in(\Sigma\backslash[-1,1])\cap(U_{\delta}(-1)\backslash U_{1/n}(-1))}\big|\Psi_{2}(n^{2}f(z))e^{2(n^{2}f(z))^{1/2}}\big|

\displaystyle\qquad{}=cn^{1/2}\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}O(n)\leqslant|y|\leqslant O(n^{2})\\
\arg y=\pm 2\pi/3\end{subarray}}\left|\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}(y)e^{2y^{1/2}}\\
\Psi_{22}(y)e^{2y^{1/2}}\end{matrix}\right|,

where in the last step we have used the fact that
n^{2}f(-\Sigma\cap U_{\delta}(-1))\subset\Gamma
for any choice of
n
, see (
B.9
). Once again, substituting in the asymptotics for
\Psi
from (
B.14
),

\displaystyle\sup_{z\in(\Sigma\cap U_{\delta}(-1))\backslash U_{1/n}(-1)}\left|\tilde{\mu}_{2}(z)\right|=O(n).

(B.21)

Lastly, it is clear from (
B.12
) that
\tilde{\mu}_{2}
is uniformly bounded for
z\in\Sigma\backslash([-1,1]\cup U_{\delta}(-1)\cup U_{\delta}(1))
. Combining this with (
B.20
) and (
B.21
), if we are at least a distance
1/n
from
\pm 1
on
\Sigma\backslash[-1,1]
, then
|\tilde{\mu}|\leqslant cn
, that is,

\displaystyle\left\|\tilde{\mu}_{2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma\backslash([-1,1]\cup U_{1/n}(-1)\cup U_{1/n}(1))}=O(n).

(B.22)

Similarly, we prove an
L^{\infty}
bound on the function
\phi(z)
: A straightforward calculation using properties (
2.3
) and (
2.4
) of Proposition
2.1
shows that

\displaystyle\inf_{z\in(\Sigma\backslash[-1,1])\cap(U_{\delta}(-1)\backslash U_{1/n}(-1))}|\phi(z)|=1+\frac{c}{\sqrt{n}},\qquad\text{ for some $c>0$}

(B.23)

and, since the contour
\Sigma\backslash([-1,1]\cup U_{\delta}(-1)\cup U_{\delta}(1))
is bounded away from the interval
[-1,1]
, property (
2.6
) of Proposition
2.1
implies

\displaystyle\inf_{z\in\Sigma\backslash([-1,1]\cup U_{\delta}(-1)\cup U_{\delta}(1))}|\phi(z)|=1+c,\qquad\text{for some fixed $c>0$}.

(B.24)

Equations (
B.23
) and (
B.24
) together imply

\displaystyle\big\|\phi^{-1}(z)\big\|_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma\backslash([-1,1]\cup U_{1/n}(1)\cup U_{1/n}(-1)))}\leqslant 1-\frac{c}{\sqrt{n}}\qquad\text{ for some $c>0$},

\displaystyle\big\|\phi^{-2n}(z)\big\|_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma\backslash([-1,1]\cup U_{1/n}(1)\cup U_{1/n}(-1)))}\leqslant c_{1}e^{-c_{2}n^{1/2}}.

(B.25)

So, using the fact that
\frac{F^{2}}{w}
is bounded, (
B.22
), and (
B.25
),

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{\mu}\left(v_{\Sigma}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}\right)\right|^{2}=\left|\left(\mathbf{0},\tilde{\mu}_{2}\right)(v_{\Sigma}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma})\right|^{2}=O\big(e^{-cn^{1/2}}\big)

uniformly for
s\in\Sigma\backslash([-1,1]\cup U_{1/n}(-1)\cup U_{1/n}(1))
and so

\displaystyle I_{3}=O\big(e^{-cn^{1/2}}\big).

(B.26)

Turning to
I_{1}
and using (
B.13
),

\displaystyle I_{1}=\int_{U_{1/n}(1)\cap\Sigma}\left|\tilde{\mu}\left(v_{\Sigma}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}\right)\right|^{2}|{\rm d}s|=\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\left|\tilde{\mu}\left(v_{\Sigma}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}\right)\right|^{2}{\rm d}s

\displaystyle=\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\!\left|R^{(n)}(s)E(s)(2\pi n)^{\sigma_{3}/2}\Psi\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)\phi^{-n\sigma_{3}}(s)\!\left(\!\begin{matrix}0&0\\
\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2\right)\phi^{-2n}\!&0\end{matrix}\right)\right|^{2}\!{\rm d}s

\displaystyle=\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\left|\left(I+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)E(s)(2\pi n)^{\sigma_{3}/2}\Psi\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)\left(\begin{matrix}0&0\\
1&0\end{matrix}\right)\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad{}\times\left|\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2\right)\phi^{-n}(s)\right|^{2}{\rm d}s

\displaystyle\leqslant cn\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)&0\\
\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)&0\end{matrix}\right)\right|^{2}\left|\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2\right|^{2}{\rm d}s.

By Proposition
2.4
,

\displaystyle\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2=O\left(\frac{1}{\log^{2}|s-1|}\right)\qquad\text{as $s\to 1$},

so for
|s-1|\leqslant\frac{1}{n}
,

\displaystyle\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2=O\left(\frac{1}{\log^{2}n}\right).

(B.27)

So

\displaystyle cn\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)&0\\
\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)&0\end{matrix}\right)\right|^{2}\left|\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2\right|^{2}{\rm d}s

\displaystyle\qquad{}\leqslant c\frac{n}{\log^{4}n}\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)&0\\
\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)&0\end{matrix}\right)\right|^{2}{\rm d}s.

Making the substitution
y=n^{2}f(s)
,

\displaystyle c\frac{n}{\log^{4}n}\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)&0\\
\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)&0\end{matrix}\right)\right|^{2}{\rm d}s

\displaystyle\qquad{}=c\frac{n}{\log^{4}n}\int_{0}^{n^{2}f(1+\frac{1}{n})}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}(y)&0\\
\Psi_{22}(y)&0\end{matrix}\right)\right|^{2}{\rm d}f^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\right).

Using (
B.7
), we see that
n^{2}f(1+\frac{1}{n})=O(n)
and
{\rm d}f^{-1}\big(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\big)=2\frac{{\rm d}y}{n^{2}}\big(1+O\big(\frac{1}{n}\big)\big)
. Therefore,

\displaystyle c\frac{n}{\log^{4}n}\int_{0}^{n^{2}f(1+\frac{1}{n})}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}(y)&0\\
\Psi_{22}(y)&0\end{matrix}\right)\right|^{2}{\rm d}f^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}\leqslant\frac{c}{n\log^{4}n}\int_{0}^{n^{2}f(1+\frac{1}{n})}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}(y)&0\\
\Psi_{22}(y)&0\end{matrix}\right)\right|^{2}{\rm d}y=\frac{c}{n\log^{4}n},

where we have used that
\Psi_{12},\Psi_{22}\in L^{2}(0,\infty)
, which is clear from (
B.14
). So we have proved

\displaystyle I_{1}=O\left(\frac{1}{n\log^{4}n}\right).

(B.28)

Similarly

\displaystyle I_{2}=\int_{(\Sigma\cap U_{1/n}(-1))\backslash[-1,1]}\left|\tilde{\mu}\left(v_{\Sigma}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}\right)\right|^{2}|{\rm d}s|=\int_{(\Sigma\cap U_{1/n}(-1))\backslash[-1,1]}\left|\tilde{\mu}\left(v_{\Sigma}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}\right)\right|^{2}|{\rm d}s|

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_2 }{}=\int_{(\Sigma\cap U_{1/n}(-1))\backslash[-1,1]}\left|\sigma_{3}R^{(n)}(-s)E(-s)(2\pi n)^{\sigma_{3}/2}\Psi\big(n^{2}f(-s)\big)\phi^{-n\sigma_{3}}(-s)\sigma_{3}\vphantom{\left(\begin{matrix}0&0\\
\left(\dfrac{F^{2}}{w}(s)-1\right)\phi^{-2n}(s)&0\end{matrix}\right)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hphantom{I_{2}=}{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}0&0\\
\left(\dfrac{F^{2}}{w}(s)-1\right)\phi^{-2n}(s)&0\end{matrix}\right)\right|^{2}|{\rm d}s|

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_2 }{}\leqslant cn\int_{(\Sigma\cap U_{1/n}(-1))\backslash[-1,1]}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\big(n^{2}f(-s)\big)&0\\
\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(-s)\big)&0\end{matrix}\right)\right|^{2}\left|\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w}(s)-1\right)\phi^{-n}(-s)\right|^{2}|{\rm d}s|

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_2 }{}\leqslant cn\int_{(\Sigma\cap U_{1/n}(-1))\backslash[-1,1]}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\big(n^{2}f(-s)\big)&0\\
\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(-s)\big)&0\end{matrix}\right)\right|^{2}\left|\frac{F^{2}}{w}(s)-1\right|^{2}|{\rm d}s|.

By Proposition
2.3
,

\displaystyle\frac{F^{2}}{w}(s)-1=O\big(|s+1|^{1/2}\big),\qquad\text{as $s\to-1$},

and so for
|s+1|\leqslant\frac{1}{n}
,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{F^{2}}{w}(s)-1\right|=O\big(n^{-1/2}\big),

(B.29)

and so

\displaystyle cn\int_{(\Sigma\cap U_{1/n}(-1))\backslash[-1,1]}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\big(n^{2}f(-s)\big)&0\\
\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(-s)\big)&0\end{matrix}\right)\right|^{2}\left|\frac{F^{2}}{w}(s)-1\right|^{2}|{\rm d}s|

\displaystyle\qquad{}\leqslant c\int_{(\Sigma\cap U_{1/n}(-1))\backslash[-1,1]}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\big(n^{2}f(-s)\big)&0\\
\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(-s)\big)&0\end{matrix}\right)\right|^{2}|{\rm d}s|.

Making the change of variables
y=n^{2}f(-s)
, and using (
B.7
),

\displaystyle c\int_{(\Sigma\cap U_{1/n}(-1))\backslash[-1,1]}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\big(n^{2}f(-s)\big)&0\\
\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(-s)\big)&0\end{matrix}\right)\right|^{2}|{\rm d}s|

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{c}{n^{2}}\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)\int_{n^{2}f(-(\Sigma\cap U_{1/n}(-1))\backslash[-1,1])}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}(y)&0\\
\Psi_{22}(y)&0\end{matrix}\right)\right|^{2}|{\rm d}y|.

Recall that
n^{2}f(-(\Sigma\cap U_{\delta}(-1)))\subset\Gamma
, see (
B.9
), therefore

\displaystyle\frac{c}{n^{2}}\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)\int_{n^{2}f(-(\Sigma\cap U_{1/n}(-1))\backslash[-1,1])}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}(y)&0\\
\Psi_{22}(y)&0\end{matrix}\right)\right|^{2}|{\rm d}y|

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{c}{n^{2}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leqslant|y|\leqslant O(n)\\
|\arg y|=2\pi/3\end{subarray}}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}(y)&0\\
\Psi_{22}(y)&0\end{matrix}\right)\right|^{2}|{\rm d}y|\leqslant\frac{c}{n^{2}},

where the asymptotics of
\Psi_{12}
,
\Psi_{22}
in (
B.14
) imply
\Psi_{12}(y),\Psi_{22}(y)\in L^{2}(C_{0})
, where
C_{0}
is the contour
|\arg y|=2\pi/3
. This proves

\displaystyle I_{2}=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right).

(B.30)

Thus combining (
B.19
), (
B.28
), (
B.30
), and (
B.26
), we see that

\displaystyle\big|\big|\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n)}\big)\big|\big|_{L^{2}(\Sigma)}^{2}=O\left(\frac{1}{n\log^{4}n}\right),

\displaystyle\big|\big|\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n)}\big)\big|\big|_{L^{2}(\Sigma)}=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{1/2}\log^{2}n}\right),

thus proving (
B.15
).

The equation (
B.16
) is a relatively straightforward consequence of (
B.15
) as follows

\displaystyle\mu-\tilde{\mu}=(1-C_{v_{\Sigma}})^{-1}I-(1-C_{\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}})^{-1}I=(1-C_{v_{\Sigma}})^{-1}(C_{v_{\Sigma}}-C_{\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}})(1-C_{\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}})^{-1}I

\displaystyle\hphantom{\mu- \tilde{\mu}}{}=(1-C_{v_{\Sigma}})^{-1}(C_{v_{\Sigma}}-C_{\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}})\tilde{\mu}=(1-C_{v_{\Sigma}})^{-1}C_{\Sigma}^{-}(\tilde{\mu}(v_{\Sigma}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma})).

Therefore, using the fact that
C_{\Sigma}^{-}
and
(1-C_{v_{\Sigma}})^{-1}
are both bounded as
L^{2}\to L^{2}
operators uniformly in
n
, we see that

\displaystyle\big\|\mu-\tilde{\mu}\big\|_{L^{2}}\leqslant\big\|(1-C_{v_{\Sigma}})^{-1}\big\|_{L^{2}\to L^{2}}\big\|C_{\Sigma}^{-}\big\|_{L^{2}\to L^{2}}\big\|\tilde{\mu}(v_{\Sigma}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma})\big\|_{L^{2}}=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{1/2}\log^{2}n}\right),

thus proving (
B.16
).

As in the proof of (
B.15
), we have that

\displaystyle\big|\big|\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n)}\big)-\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}\big)\big|\big|_{L^{2}(\Sigma)}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\left(\int_{\Sigma\cap U_{1/n}(1)}+\int_{\Sigma\cap U_{\frac{1}{n}}(-1)}+\int_{\Sigma\backslash(U_{\frac{1}{n}}(-1)\cup U_{\frac{1}{n}}(1))}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}\times\big|\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n)}\big)-\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}\big)\big|^{2}|{\rm d}s|=I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3},

(B.31)

and the same estimates as before imply

\displaystyle I_{3}=O\big(e^{-cn^{1/2}}\big).

(B.32)

For
I_{1}
, we see that

\displaystyle I_{1}=\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\big|\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-\tilde{v}^{(n)}_{\Sigma}\big)-\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}-\tilde{v}^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}\big)\big|^{2}{\rm d}s

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_1 }{}=\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\Bigg|\left(I+\frac{R_{1}(s)}{n}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)E(s)(2\pi n)^{\sigma_{3}/2}\Psi_{2}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)\phi^{n}(s)

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_1 =}{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}0&0\\
\left(\dfrac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\dfrac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2\right)\phi^{-2n}&0\end{matrix}\right)-\big((n)\leftrightarrow(n+1)\big)\Bigg|^{2}{\rm d}s

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_1 }{}=\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\Bigg|\left(I+\frac{R_{1}(s)}{n}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)E(s)(2\pi n)^{\sigma_{3}/2}\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)&0\\
\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)&0\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_1 =}{}\times\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2\right)\phi^{-n}-\big((n)\leftrightarrow(n+1)\big)\Bigg|^{2}{\rm d}s.

(B.33)

Making the substitution
y=n^{2}f(s)
, and defining
s=s_{n}(y)=f^{-1}_{1}\big(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\big)
, where
f_{1}^{-1}
denotes the inverse of
f_{1}
in the neighborhood
B
where it is conformal, (
B.33
) becomes

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{n^{2}f\left(1+\frac{1}{n}\right)}\left|\left(I+\frac{R_{1}(s)}{n}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)E(s)(2\pi n)^{\sigma_{3}/2}\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}(y)&0\\
\Psi_{22}(y)&0\end{matrix}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2\right)e^{-2y^{1/2}}-\left.\left(I+\frac{R_{1}(s)}{n+1}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)E(s)(2\pi(n+1))^{\sigma_{3}/2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\left(\dfrac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y\right)&0\vskip 2.84526pt\\
\Psi_{22}\left(\dfrac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y\right)&0\end{matrix}\right)\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2\right)e^{-2\frac{n+1}{n}y^{1/2}}\right|^{2}{\rm d}f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=2\pi n\int_{0}^{n^{2}f\left(1+\frac{1}{n}\right)}\left|\left(I+\frac{R_{1}(s)}{n}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)E(s)\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}\end{matrix}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}(y)&0\\
\Psi_{22}(y)&0\end{matrix}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2\right)e^{-2y^{1/2}}

\displaystyle\left.\qquad{}-\left(I+\frac{R_{1}(s)}{n+1}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)E(s)\left(\begin{matrix}\sqrt{\dfrac{n+1}{n}}&0\\
0&\dfrac{1}{2\pi\sqrt{n(n+1)}}\end{matrix}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\left(\dfrac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y\right)&0\vskip 2.84526pt\\
\Psi_{22}\left(\dfrac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y\right)&0\end{matrix}\right)\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2\right)e^{-2\frac{n+1}{n}y^{1/2}}\right|^{2}{\rm d}f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\right).

(B.34)

First note that
n^{2}f(1+\frac{1}{n})=O(n)
and for
|y|\leqslant cn
,
{\rm d}f_{1}^{-1}\big(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\big)=\frac{{\rm d}y}{n^{2}}\big(1+O\big(\frac{1}{n}\big)\big)
. As in (
B.27
),
\big|\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2\big|\leqslant\frac{c}{\log^{2}n}
. Therefore (
B.34
) is

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{O(n)}\left|\left(I+\frac{R_{1}(s)}{n}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)E(s)\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}\end{matrix}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}(y)&0\\
\Psi_{22}(y)&0\end{matrix}\right)e^{-2y^{1/2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad{}-\left(I+\frac{R_{1}(s)}{n+1}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)E(s)\left(\begin{matrix}2\pi\sqrt{\dfrac{n+1}{n}}&0\\
0&\dfrac{1}{2\pi\sqrt{n(n+1)}}\end{matrix}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\left(\dfrac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y\right)&0\vskip 2.84526pt\\
\Psi_{22}\left(\dfrac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y\right)&0\end{matrix}\right)e^{-2\frac{n+1}{n}y^{1/2}}\right|^{2}{\rm d}y\,O\left(\frac{1}{n\log^{4}n}\right).

(B.35)

Now
R_{1}
and
E
are uniformly bounded functions, and
\Psi_{12}
,
\Psi_{22}
are
L^{2}(0,\infty)
functions. Therefore, performing the subtraction in (
B.35
) term by term, (
B.35
) is

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{O(n)}\left|O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}(y)&0\\
\Psi_{22}(y)&0\end{matrix}\right)e^{-2y^{1/2}}\right|^{2}{\rm d}y\,O\left(\frac{1}{n\log^{4}n}\right)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{O(n)}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}(y)&0\\
\Psi_{22}(y)&0\end{matrix}\right)e^{-2y^{1/2}}-\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\left(\dfrac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y\right)&0\vskip 2.84526pt\\
\Psi_{22}\left(\dfrac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y\right)&0\end{matrix}\right)e^{-2\frac{n+1}{n}y^{1/2}}\right|^{2}{\rm d}y\,O\left(\frac{1}{n\log^{4}n}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{3}\log^{4}n}\right)+\int_{0}^{O(n)}\left|\int_{y}^{\frac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y}\frac{{\rm d}}{{\rm d}x}\left(\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}(x)&0\\
\Psi_{22}(x)&0\end{matrix}\right)e^{-2x^{1/2}}\right){\rm d}x\right|^{2}{\rm d}y\,O\left(\frac{1}{n\log^{4}n}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{3}\log^{4}n}\right)+\int_{0}^{O(n)}\left|\frac{(n+1)^{2}-n^{2}}{n^{2}}y\sup_{\left(y,\frac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y\right)}\frac{{\rm d}}{{\rm d}x}\left(\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}(x)&0\\
\Psi_{22}(x)&0\end{matrix}\right)e^{-2x^{1/2}}\right)\right|^{2}{\rm d}y

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times O\left(\frac{1}{n\log^{4}n}\right).

(B.36)

Now,
\Psi_{2}(x)=O(\log x)
and
\Psi_{2}^{\prime}(x)=O\big(\frac{1}{x}\big)
as
x\to 0
and
\Psi_{2},\Psi_{2}^{\prime}(x)=O\big(x^{1/4}e^{-2x^{1/2}}\big)
as
x\to\infty
, see (
B.14
). As a consequence,

\displaystyle y\sup_{\left(y,\frac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y\right)}\frac{{\rm d}}{{\rm d}x}\left(\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}(x)&0\\
\Psi_{22}(x)&0\end{matrix}\right)e^{-2x^{1/2}}\right)=\begin{cases}O(1)&\text{for all $y$},\\
O\big(y^{1/4}e^{-4y^{1/2}}\big)&\text{as $y\to\infty$},\end{cases}

and thus

\displaystyle\left\|y\sup_{\left(y,\frac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y\right)}\frac{{\rm d}}{{\rm d}x}\left(\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}(x)&0\\
\Psi_{22}(x)&0\end{matrix}\right)e^{-2x^{1/2}}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(0,\infty)}=O(1),

and so

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{O(n)}\left|\frac{(n+1)^{2}-n^{2}}{n^{2}}y\sup_{\left(y,\frac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y\right)}\frac{{\rm d}}{{\rm d}x}\left(\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}(x)&0\\
\Psi_{22}(x)&0\end{matrix}\right)e^{-2x^{1/2}}\right){\rm d}x\right|^{2}{\rm d}y

\displaystyle\qquad{}\leqslant\frac{c}{n^{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\left|y\sup_{\left(y,\frac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y\right)}\frac{{\rm d}}{{\rm d}x}\left(\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}(x)&0\\
\Psi_{22}(x)&0\end{matrix}\right)e^{-2x^{1/2}}\right){\rm d}x\right|^{2}{\rm d}y=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right),

and hence, by (
B.36
),

\displaystyle I_{1}=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{3}\log^{4}n}\right).

(B.37)

A similar calculation shows

\displaystyle I_{2}=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{4}}\right).

(B.38)

Thus combining (
B.31
), (
B.37
), (
B.38
), and (
B.32
), we see that

\displaystyle\big|\big|\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n)}\big)-\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}\big)\big|\big|_{L^{2}(\Sigma)}^{2}=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{3}\log^{4}n}\right),

\displaystyle\big|\big|\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n)}\big)-\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}\big)\big|\big|_{L^{2}(\Sigma)}=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{3/2}\log^{2}n}\right),

thus proving (
B.17
).

To move from (
B.17
) to (
B.18
), we proceed as follows

\displaystyle\big(\mu^{(n)}-\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big)-\big(\mu^{(n+1)}-\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\big(\big(1-C_{v^{(n)}_{\Sigma}}\big)^{-1}I-\big(1-C_{\tilde{v}^{(n)}_{\Sigma}}\big)^{-1}I\big)-\big(\big(1-C_{v^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}}\big)^{-1}I-\big(1-C_{v^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}}\big)^{-1}I\big)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\big(1-C_{v^{(n)}_{\Sigma}}\big)^{-1}C_{\Sigma}^{-}\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n)}\big)-\big(1-C_{v^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}}\big)^{-1}C_{\Sigma}^{-}\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\big(\big(1-C_{v^{(n)}_{\Sigma}}\big)^{-1}-\big(1-C_{v^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}}\big)^{-1}\big)C_{\Sigma}^{-}\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n)}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}+\big(1-C_{v^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}}\big)^{-1}C_{\Sigma}^{-}\big(\tilde{\mu}^{n}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n)}\big)-\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}\big)\big).

(B.39)

Now, by (
B.17
) and the boundedness of the operators
C_{\Sigma}^{-}
and
(1-C_{v^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}})^{-1}
, we see that

\displaystyle\big\|\big(1-C_{v^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}}\big)^{-1}C_{\Sigma}^{-}\big(\tilde{\mu}^{n}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n)}\big)-\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}\big)\big)\big\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{3/2}\log^{2}n}\right)

(B.40)

and

\displaystyle\big(\big(1-C_{v^{(n)}_{\Sigma}}\big)^{-1}-\big(1-C_{v^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}}\big)^{-1}\big)C_{\Sigma}^{-}\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n)}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\big(\big(1-C_{v^{(n)}_{\Sigma}}\big)^{-1}\big(C_{v^{(n)}_{\Sigma}}-C_{v^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}}\big)\big(1-C_{v^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}}\big)^{-1}\big)C_{\Sigma}^{-}\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n)}\big).

Now

\displaystyle\big\|C_{v^{(n)}_{\Sigma}}-C_{v^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}}\big\|_{L^{2}\to L^{2}}=\|C_{\Sigma}^{-}\|_{L^{2}\to L^{2}}\big\|v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-v_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}\big\|_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)},

where

\displaystyle v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-v_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}=\begin{cases}\left(\begin{matrix}0&0\\
\dfrac{F^{2}}{w}(z)\phi^{-2n}(z)\big(1-\phi^{-2}(z)\big)&0\end{matrix}\right)&\text{for $z\in\Sigma\backslash[-1,1]$},\\
0&\text{for $z\in[-1,1]$}.\end{cases}

Therefore

\displaystyle\big\|v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-v_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}\big\|_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\leqslant c\big\|\phi^{-2n}\big(1-\phi^{-2}\big)\big\|_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma\backslash[-1,1])}.

(B.41)

When
z\in(\Sigma\backslash[-1,1])\cap U_{\delta}(1)
,
\phi(z)=1+\sqrt{2}(z-1)^{1/2}+O(z-1)
, letting
y=n^{2}(z-1)
,

\displaystyle\phi^{-2n}(z)\big(1-\phi^{-2}\big)(z)=\left(1+\sqrt{2}\frac{y^{1/2}}{n}+O\left(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\right)\right)^{-2n}\left(2\sqrt{2}\frac{y^{1/2}}{n}+O\left(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\hphantom{\phi^{-2n} (z)\big(1 - \phi^{-2}\big)(z)}{}=\frac{2\sqrt{2}y^{1/2}}{n}e^{-2\sqrt{2}y^{1/2}}\left(1+O\left(\frac{y^{1/2}}{n}\right)\right).

Since
z\in(\Sigma\backslash[-1,1])\cap U_{\delta}(1)
,
\operatorname{Re}(y^{1/2})\geqslant\epsilon>0
. Therefore
y^{1/2}e^{-2\sqrt{2}y^{1/2}}\leqslant c<\infty
, so

\displaystyle\big|\phi^{-2n}(z)\big(1-\phi^{-2}\big)(z)\big|\leqslant\frac{c}{n},\qquad\text{for $z\in(\Sigma\backslash[-1,1])\cap U_{\delta}(1)$}.

The same argument shows the same bound around the point
-1
. Since
|\phi(z)|\geqslant 1+\delta>1
when
z\in\Sigma\backslash([-1,1]\cup U_{\delta}(1)\cup U_{\delta}(-1))
,

\displaystyle\left|\phi^{-2n}(z)\big(1-\phi^{-2}\big)(z)\right|\leqslant ce^{-n\delta},\qquad\text{for $z\in\Sigma\backslash([-1,1]\cup U_{\delta}(1)\cup U_{\delta}(-1))$}

Therefore,

\displaystyle\big\|\phi^{-2n}\big(1-\phi^{-2}\big)\big\|_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma\backslash[-1,1])}=O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right),

which, combined with (
B.41
), implies

\displaystyle\big\|v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-v_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}\big\|_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}=O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right),\qquad\big\|C_{v^{(n)}_{\Sigma}}-C_{v^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}}\big\|_{L^{2}\to L^{2}}=O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

Therefore

\displaystyle\big\|\big(\big(1-C_{v^{(n)}_{\Sigma}}\big)^{-1}-\big(1-C_{v^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}}\big)^{-1}\big)C_{\Sigma}^{-}\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n)}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma)}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\big\|\big(\big(1-C_{v^{(n)}_{\Sigma}}\big)^{-1}\big(C_{v^{(n)}_{\Sigma}}-C_{v^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}}\big)\big(1-C_{v^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}}\big)^{-1}\big)C_{\Sigma}^{-}\big\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma)\to L^{2}(\Sigma)}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}\times\big\|\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n)}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma)},

\displaystyle\big\|\big(C_{v^{(n)}_{\Sigma}}-C_{v^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma)\to L^{2}(\Sigma)}\big\|\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}^{(n)}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma)}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)O\left(\frac{1}{n^{1/2}\log^{2}n}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{3/2}\log^{2}n}\right).

(B.42)

So, combining (
B.39
), (
B.40
), and (
B.42
), we see that

\displaystyle\big|\big|\big(\mu^{(n)}-\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big)-\big(\mu^{(n+1)}-\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}\big)\big|\big|_{L^{2}(\Sigma)}=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{3/2}\log^{2}n}\right)

as desired.
∎

Appendix C
Leading order asymptotics of the difference formula

In this appendix we calculate the integrals that arise in Proposition
5.2
.

C.1
Some preliminaries

Proposition C.1
.

The following bounds hold

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{1+1/n}\left|\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2\right)\right||{\rm d}s|=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{2}n}\right),

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{1+1/n}\left|\Psi_{22}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2\right)\right||{\rm d}s|=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{2}n}\right),

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{1+1/n}\left|\Psi_{12}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2\right)\right||{\rm d}s|=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{2}n}\right),

(C.1)

furthermore

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{1+1/n}\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006pts=\frac{3}{16\pi in^{2}\log^{2}n}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\right).

(C.2)

Proof.

Recall that by Proposition
2.4
, as
s\to 1
,

\displaystyle\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2=-\frac{3\pi^{2}}{\log^{2}\frac{2k}{1-s}}+O\left(\frac{1}{\log^{3}|1-s|}\right).

In particular, note that for
1\leqslant s\leqslant 1+1/n
, that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2\right|=O\left(\frac{1}{\log^{2}n}\right).

Therefore

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{1+1/n}\left|\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2\right)\right||{\rm d}s|

\displaystyle\qquad{}\leqslant\frac{c}{\log^{2}n}\int_{1}^{1+1/n}\!\big|\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)\big|{\rm d}s=\frac{c}{\log^{2}n}\int_{0}^{n^{2}f(1+1/n)}\!\big|\Psi_{12}^{2}(y)\big|\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+O\big(n^{-3}\big)\right){\rm d}y

\displaystyle\qquad{}\leqslant\frac{c}{n^{2}\log^{2}n}\int_{0}^{n^{2}f(1+1/n)}\big|\Psi_{12}^{2}(y)\big|{\rm d}y\leqslant\frac{c}{n^{2}\log^{2}n}\int_{0}^{\infty}\big|\Psi_{12}^{2}(y)\big|{\rm d}y\leqslant\frac{c}{n^{2}\log^{2}n},

where we have used the fact that

\displaystyle f(z)=\frac{z-1}{2}+O\big((z-1)^{2}\big),

and that
\Psi_{12}(y)\in L^{2}(0,\infty)
. Similar arguments show the other estimates in (
C.1
).

Paying more attention, we can make the calculation (
C.2
),

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{1+1/n}\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(s)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(s)-2\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006pts

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\int_{1}^{1+1/n}\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)\left(-\frac{3\pi^{2}}{\log^{2}(2k/|1-s|)}+O\left(\frac{1}{\log^{3}|1-s|}\right)\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006pts.

Letting
s=1+\frac{t}{n^{2}}
,

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{1+1/n}\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(s)\big)\left(-\frac{3\pi^{2}}{\log^{2}(2k/|1-s|)}+O\left(\frac{1}{\log^{3}|1-s|}\right)\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006pts

\displaystyle=-\frac{3\pi^{2}}{n^{2}}\int_{0}^{n}\Psi_{12}^{2}\left(n^{2}f\left(1+\frac{t}{n^{2}}\right)\right)\left(\frac{1}{\log^{2}(2kn^{2}/t)}+O\left(\frac{1}{\log^{3}n}\right)\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptt

\displaystyle=-\frac{3\pi^{2}}{n^{2}}\int_{0}^{n}\Psi_{12}^{2}\left(n^{2}f\left(1+\frac{t}{n^{2}}\right)\right)\left(\frac{1}{\left(2\log n+\log\frac{2k}{t}\right)^{2}}+O\left(\frac{1}{\log n}\right)\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptt

\displaystyle=-\frac{3\pi^{2}}{4n^{2}\log^{2}n}\int_{0}^{n}\Psi_{12}^{2}\left(n^{2}f\left(1+\frac{t}{n^{2}}\right)\right)\left(\frac{1}{\left(1+\frac{\log\frac{2k}{t}}{2\log n}\right)^{2}}+O\left(\frac{1}{\log n}\right)\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptt

\displaystyle=-\frac{3\pi^{2}}{4n^{2}\log^{2}n}\int_{0}^{n}\Psi_{12}^{2}\left(n^{2}f\left(1+\frac{t}{n^{2}}\right)\right)\left(\frac{1}{1+\frac{\log\frac{2k}{t}}{\log n}+\frac{\log^{2}\frac{2k}{t}}{4\log^{2}n}}+O\left(\frac{1}{\log n}\right)\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptt

(C.3)

\displaystyle=-\frac{3\pi^{2}}{4n^{2}\log^{2}n}\int_{0}^{n}\Psi_{12}^{2}\left(n^{2}f\left(1+\frac{t}{n^{2}}\right)\right)\left(1-\frac{1}{\log n}\frac{\log\frac{2k}{t}+\frac{\log^{2}\frac{2k}{t}}{4\log n}}{1+\frac{\log\frac{2k}{t}}{\log n}+\frac{\log^{2}\frac{2k}{t}}{4\log^{2}n}}+O\left(\frac{1}{\log n}\right)\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptt.

Note that for
0\leqslant t\leqslant n
,
-\infty<\log\frac{t}{2k}<\log n
, and

\displaystyle\frac{\log\frac{2k}{t}}{2\log n}>-\frac{1}{2},

so

\displaystyle 1+\frac{\log\frac{2k}{t}}{\log n}+\frac{\log^{2}\frac{2k}{t}}{4\log^{2}n}=\left(1+\frac{\log\frac{2k}{t}}{2\log n}\right)^{2}\geqslant\frac{1}{4},

and therefore

\displaystyle\frac{3\pi^{2}}{4n^{2}\log^{2}n}\int_{0}^{n}\left|\Psi_{12}^{2}\left(n^{2}f\left(1+\frac{t}{n^{2}}\right)\right)\left(\frac{1}{\log n}\frac{\log\frac{2k}{t}+\frac{\log^{2}\frac{2k}{t}}{4\log n}}{1+\frac{\log\frac{2k}{t}}{\log n}+\frac{\log^{2}\frac{2k}{t}}{4\log^{2}n}}+O\left(\frac{1}{\log n}\right)\right)\right|{\rm d}t

\displaystyle\qquad{}\leqslant\frac{c}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\int_{0}^{n}\left|\Psi_{12}^{2}\left(n^{2}f\left(1+\frac{t}{n^{2}}\right)\right)\right|\left(4\left|\log\frac{2k}{t}\right|+4\left|\log^{2}\frac{2k}{t}\right|\right){\rm d}t.

Letting
y=n^{2}f(1+t/n^{2})
, then
{\rm d}y=-f^{\prime}(t/n^{2}){\rm d}t=-\big(\frac{1}{2}+O\big(\frac{1}{n}\big)\big){\rm d}t
and
t=2y\big(1+O\big(\frac{1}{n}\big)\big)
, so

\displaystyle\frac{c}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\int_{0}^{n}\left|\Psi_{12}^{2}\left(n^{2}f\left(1+\frac{t}{n^{2}}\right)\right)\right|\left(4\left|\log\frac{2k}{t}\right|+4\left|\log^{2}\frac{2k}{t}\right|\right){\rm d}t

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{c}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\int_{0}^{n^{2}f(1+1/n)}\left|\Psi_{12}^{2}(y)\right|\left(\left|\log\frac{k}{y}\right|+\left|\log^{2}\frac{k}{y}\right|+O\left(\frac{1}{n}+\frac{\log y}{n}\right)\right){\rm d}y

\displaystyle\qquad{}\leqslant\frac{c}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\int_{0}^{\infty}\left|\Psi_{12}^{2}(y)\right|\left(\left|\log y\right|+\left|\log^{2}y\right|\right){\rm d}y\leqslant\frac{c}{n^{2}\log^{3}n},

(C.4)

where the integrability of (
C.4
) follows from the asymptotics of
\Psi_{12}(y)
in (
B.14
). Thus

\displaystyle\frac{3\pi^{2}}{4n^{2}\log^{2}n}\int_{0}^{n}\left|\Psi_{12}^{2}\left(n^{2}f\left(1+\frac{t}{n^{2}}\right)\right)\left(\frac{1}{\log n}\frac{\log\frac{2k}{|t|}+\frac{\log^{2}\frac{2k}{|t|}}{4\log n}}{1+\frac{\log\frac{2k}{|t|}}{\log n}+\frac{\log^{2}\frac{2k}{|t|}}{4\log^{2}n}}+O\left(\frac{1}{\log n}\right)\right)\right|{\rm d}t

\displaystyle\qquad{}=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\right).

Therefore, picking up from (
C.3
),

\displaystyle-\frac{3\pi^{2}}{4n^{2}\log^{2}n}\int_{0}^{n}\Psi_{12}^{2}\left(n^{2}f\left(1+\frac{t}{n^{2}}\right)\right)\left(1-\frac{1}{\log n}\frac{\log\frac{2k}{|t|}+\frac{\log^{2}\frac{2k}{|t|}}{4\log n}}{1+\frac{\log\frac{2k}{|t|}}{\log n}+\frac{\log^{2}\frac{2k}{|t|}}{4\log^{2}n}}+O\left(\frac{1}{\log n}\right)\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptt

\displaystyle\qquad{}=-\frac{3\pi^{2}}{4n^{2}\log^{2}n}\int_{0}^{n}\Psi_{12}^{2}\left(n^{2}f\left(1+\frac{t}{n^{2}}\right)\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptt+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=-\frac{3\pi^{2}}{4n^{2}\log^{2}n}\int_{0}^{n}\left(-\frac{1}{\pi^{2}}\right)K_{0}^{2}\left(2nf^{1/2}\left(1+\frac{t}{n^{2}}\right)\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptt+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{3}{4n^{2}\log^{2}n}\int_{0}^{n}K_{0}^{2}\left(2nf^{1/2}\left(1+\frac{t}{n^{2}}\right)\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptt+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\right).

Making the change of variables
y=n^{2}f\big(1+\frac{t}{n^{2}}\big)
and noting that this implies
{\rm d}t=\big(2+O\big(\frac{1}{n}\big)\big){\rm d}y
,

\displaystyle\frac{3}{4n^{2}\log^{2}n}\int_{0}^{n}K_{0}^{2}\left(2nf^{1/2}\left(1+\frac{t}{n^{2}}\right)\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptt+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{3}{2n^{2}\log^{2}n}\int_{0}^{n^{2}f(1+1/n)}K_{0}^{2}\big(2y^{1/2}\big){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006pty+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\right).

K_{0}(y)
is exponentially decaying for
y>>0
, see
[
1
, formula (9.7.2)]
. Therefore

\displaystyle\frac{3}{2n^{2}\log^{2}n}\int_{0}^{n^{2}f(1+1/n)}K_{0}^{2}\big(2y^{1/2}\big){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006pty+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{3}{2n^{2}\log^{2}n}\int_{0}^{\infty}K_{0}^{2}\big(2y^{1/2}\big){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006pty+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{3}{n^{2}\log^{2}n}\int_{0}^{\infty}K_{0}^{2}\left(2u\right)u{\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptu+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{3}{2\pi i}\frac{1}{8n^{2}\log^{2}n}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\right)=\frac{3}{16\pi in^{2}\log^{2}n}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\right),

where we have used the fact that
\int_{0}^{\infty}K_{0}^{2}(v)v{\rm d}v=\frac{1}{2}
, see
[
12
]
. We have proved (
C.2
).
∎

Additionally, we have the following matrix calculation:

Proposition C.2
.

\displaystyle E(1)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(\begin{matrix}1&-i\\
-i&1\end{matrix}\right).

(C.5)

Proof.

\displaystyle E(z)=\left(\begin{matrix}\dfrac{a+a^{-1}}{2}&\dfrac{a-a^{-1}}{2i}\\
\dfrac{a-a^{-1}}{-2i}&\dfrac{a+a^{-1}}{2}\end{matrix}\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(\begin{matrix}1&-i\\
-i&1\end{matrix}\right)f^{\sigma_{3}/4}

\displaystyle\hphantom{E(z)}{}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(\begin{matrix}\dfrac{a+a^{-1}}{2}&\dfrac{a-a^{-1}}{2i}\\
\dfrac{a-a^{-1}}{-2i}&\dfrac{a+a^{-1}}{2}\end{matrix}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}f^{1/4}(z)&-if^{-1/4}(z)\\
-f^{1/4}(z)&f^{-1/4}(z)\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\hphantom{E(z)}{}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(\begin{matrix}a^{-1}f^{1/4}(z)&-iaf^{-1/4}(z)\\
-ia^{-1}f^{1/4}(z)&af^{-1/4}(z)\end{matrix}\right).

Now

\displaystyle af^{-1/4}=\frac{(z-1)^{1/4}}{(z+1)^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\big(\frac{1}{2}(z-1)+O\big((z-1)^{2}\big)\big)^{1/4}}=1+O(z-1).

So
af^{-1/4}(1)=1
.

Therefore, we see that

\displaystyle E(1)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(\begin{matrix}1&-i\\
-i&1\end{matrix}\right)

as desired.
∎

C.2
The first integral

Proposition C.3
.

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma}\tilde{\mu}(z)(v_{\Sigma}(z)-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}(z))\tilde{\mu}^{-1}(z){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz=\frac{3}{16n\log^{2}n}\left(\begin{matrix}1&-i\\
-i&-1\end{matrix}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n\log^{3}n}\right).

(C.6)

Proof.

As in the proof of Proposition
B.2
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma}\tilde{\mu}(z)(v_{\Sigma}(z)-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}(z))\tilde{\mu}^{-1}(z){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\left(\int_{\Sigma\cap U_{1/n}(1)}+\int_{\Sigma\cap U_{1/n}(-1)}+\int_{\Sigma\backslash(U_{1/n}(1)\cup U_{1/n}(-1))}\right)\tilde{\mu}(z)(v_{\Sigma}(z)-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}(z))\tilde{\mu}^{-1}(z){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz

\displaystyle\qquad{}=I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}.

The same argument as Proposition
B.2
, gives

\displaystyle I_{3}=O\big(e^{-cn^{1/2}}\big).

For
I_{1}

\displaystyle I_{1}=-\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\tilde{\mu}(z)(v_{\Sigma}(z)-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma}(z))\tilde{\mu}^{-1}(z){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_1}{}=-\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)E(z)(2\pi n)^{\sigma_{3}/2}\Psi(n^{2}f(z))\phi^{-n\sigma_{3}}(z)

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_1=}{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}0&0\\
\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(z)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(z)-2\right)\phi^{-2n}(z)&0\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_1=}{}\times\phi^{n\sigma_{3}}(z)\Psi^{-1}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)(2\pi n)^{-\sigma_{3}/2}E^{-1}(z)\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_1}{}=-\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)E(z)(2\pi n)^{\sigma_{3}/2}\Psi(n^{2}f(z))\left(\begin{matrix}0&0\\
1&0\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_1=}{}\times\Psi^{-1}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)(2\pi n)^{-\sigma_{3}/2}E^{-1}(z)\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(z)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(z)-2\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_1}{}=-\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)E(z)(2\pi n)^{\sigma_{3}/2}\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)&-\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\\
\Psi_{22}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)&-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_1=}{}\times(2\pi n)^{-\sigma_{3}/2}E^{-1}(z)\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)\left[\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(z)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(z)-2\right)\right]{\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_1}{}=-2\pi n\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)E(z)\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}\end{matrix}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)&-\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\\
\Psi_{22}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)&-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_1=}{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}&0\\
0&1\end{matrix}\right)E^{-1}(z)\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)\left[\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(z)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(z)-2\right)\right]{\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz.

(C.7)

Now, note that by the
n
-independence and analyticity of the functions
E(z)
,
E^{-1}(z)
, for
z\in(1,1+1/n)
,

\displaystyle E(z)=E(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right),\qquad E^{-1}(z)=E^{-1}(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right),

and clearly

\displaystyle\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&\frac{1}{2\pi n}\end{matrix}\right)=\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&0\end{matrix}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

Therefore, subtracting term by term in (
C.7
), (
C.7
) becomes

\displaystyle-2\pi n\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}E(1)\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&0\end{matrix}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)&-\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\\
\Psi_{22}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)&-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\end{matrix}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}0&0\\
0&1\end{matrix}\right)E^{-1}(1)

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times\left[\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(z)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(z)-2\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz\right]+2\pi n\mathcal{E},

(C.8)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}=O\!\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)O\!\left\|\!\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\!\!&-\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\\
\Psi_{22}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\!\!&-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\end{matrix}\right)\!\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(z)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(z)-2\right)\!\right\|_{L^{1}(1,1+1/n)},

which, by (
C.1
), yields

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{3}\log^{2}n}\right).

So (
C.8
) becomes

\displaystyle-2\pi n\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}E(1)\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&0\end{matrix}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)&-\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\\
\Psi_{22}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)&-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\end{matrix}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}0&0\\
0&1\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}\times E^{-1}(1)\left[\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(z)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(z)-2\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz\right]+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{2}n}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=-2\pi n\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}E(1)\left(\begin{matrix}0&-\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\\
0&0\end{matrix}\right)E^{-1}(1)\left[\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(z)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(z)-2\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{2}n}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=2\pi n\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}E(1)\left(\begin{matrix}0&1\\
0&0\end{matrix}\right)E^{-1}(1)\left[\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(z)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(z)-2\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{2}n}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=2\pi nE(1)\left(\begin{matrix}0&1\\
0&0\end{matrix}\right)E^{-1}(1)\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(z)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(z)-2\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{2}n}\right),

which, by (
C.2
), is

\displaystyle 2\pi nE(1)\left(\begin{matrix}0&1\\
0&0\end{matrix}\right)E^{-1}(1)\left(\frac{3}{16\pi in^{2}\log^{2}n}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{3}{8in\log^{2}n}E(1)\left(\begin{matrix}0&1\\
0&0\end{matrix}\right)E^{-1}(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n\log^{3}n}\right).

Substituting in (
C.5
),

\displaystyle\frac{3}{8in\log^{2}n}E(1)\left(\begin{matrix}0&1\\
0&0\end{matrix}\right)E^{-1}(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n\log^{3}n}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{3}{8in\log^{2}n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(\begin{matrix}1&-i\\
-i&1\end{matrix}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}0&1\\
0&0\end{matrix}\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(\begin{matrix}1&i\\
i&1\end{matrix}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n\log^{3}n}\right)

(C.9)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{3}{16in\log^{2}n}\left(\begin{matrix}i&1\\
1&-i\end{matrix}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n\log^{3}n}\right)=\frac{3}{16n\log^{2}n}\left(\begin{matrix}1&-i\\
-i&-1\end{matrix}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n\log^{3}n}\right),

which is the right-hand side of (
C.6
).

All that remains is to show that
I_{2}
is of lower order. Following the same steps as the lead up to (
C.7
),

\displaystyle I_{2}=-2\pi n\int_{(\Sigma\backslash[-1,1])\cap U_{1/n}(-1)}\sigma_{3}\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)E(-z)\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\left(\begin{matrix}-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)&\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)\\
-\Psi_{22}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)&\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\left(\begin{matrix}\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}&0\\
0&1\end{matrix}\right)E^{-1}(-z)\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)\sigma_{3}\left[\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w}(z)-1\right)\right]{\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz.

Therefore,

\displaystyle|I_{2}|\leqslant cn\int_{(\Sigma\backslash[-1,1])\cap U_{1/n}(-1)}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\left(n^{2}f(-z)\right)&\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)\\
-\Psi_{22}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)&\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)\end{matrix}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\hphantom{|I_2 |\leqslant}{}\times\left|\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w}(z)-1\right)\right||{\rm d}z|.

As in (
B.29
), for
|z+1|\leqslant\frac{1}{n}
,

\displaystyle\left|\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w}(z)-1\right)\right|=O\big(n^{-1/2}\big),

and so

\displaystyle|I_{2}|\leqslant cn^{1/2}\int_{(\Sigma\backslash[-1,1])\cap U_{1/n}(-1)}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\left(n^{2}f(-z)\right)&\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)\\
-\Psi_{22}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)&\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)\end{matrix}\right)\right||{\rm d}z|.

Letting
y=n^{2}f(-z)
, in which case we have, as before,
{\rm d}z=\frac{2{\rm d}y}{n^{2}}\big(1+O\big(\frac{1}{n}\big)\big)
, and recalling that
n^{2}f(-(\Sigma\cap U_{1/n}(-1)))\subset\Gamma
,

\displaystyle|I_{2}|\leqslant cn^{-3/2}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leqslant|y|\leqslant O(n)\\
\arg y=\pm 2\pi/3\end{subarray}}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}(y)&\Psi_{12}^{2}(y)\\
-\Psi_{22}^{2}(y)&\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}(y)\end{matrix}\right)\right||{\rm d}y|=O\big(n^{-3/2}\big),

where we have used the fact that
\Psi_{12},\Psi_{22}\in L^{2}(C_{0})
, where
C_{0}
is the contour
|\arg y|=2\pi/3
, see (
B.14
). Therefore we see that

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma}\tilde{\mu}(v_{\Sigma}-\tilde{v}_{\Sigma})\tilde{\mu}^{-1}{\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz=I_{1}+O\big(n^{-3/2}\big)=\frac{3}{16n\log^{2}n}\left(\begin{matrix}1&-i\\
-i&-1\end{matrix}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n\log^{3}n}\right),

where we have used (
C.9
). Thus we have proved Proposition
C.3
.
∎

C.3
The second integral

Proposition C.4
.

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma}\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}(z)\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}(z)-\tilde{v}^{(n)}_{\Sigma}(z)\big)\big(\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big)^{-1}(z){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}-\int_{\Sigma}\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}(z)\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}(z)-\tilde{v}^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}(z)\big)\big(\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}\big)^{-1}(z){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{3}{16n^{2}\log^{2}n}\left(\begin{matrix}1&-i\\
-i&-1\end{matrix}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\right).

(C.10)

Proof.

While not sufficient to prove the above, note that

\displaystyle\frac{3}{16n\log^{2}n}\left(\begin{matrix}1&-i\\
-i&-1\end{matrix}\right)-\frac{3}{16(n+1)\log^{2}(n+1)}\left(\begin{matrix}1&-i\\
-i&-1\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{3}{16n^{2}\log^{2}n}\left(\begin{matrix}1&-i\\
-i&-1\end{matrix}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\right).

So this proposition essentially asserts that the leading order of the difference (
C.10
) is the difference of the leading orders of the two terms, which are computed in (
C.6
).

We have, as before,

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma}\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}(z)\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}(z)-\tilde{v}^{(n)}_{\Sigma}(z)\big)\big(\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big)^{-1}(z){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz

\displaystyle\qquad{}-\int_{\Sigma}\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}(z)\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}(z)-\tilde{v}^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}(z)\big)\big(\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}\big)^{-1}(z){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz=I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3},

where
I_{1}
,
I_{2}
,
I_{3}
refer to the difference of the integrals of the above over
[1,1+\delta]
,
\Sigma\cap U_{1/n}(-1)
, and
\Sigma\backslash(U_{1/n}(1)\cap U_{1/n}(-1))
respectively. The same argument as in Proposition
B.2
shows

\displaystyle I_{3}=O\big(e^{-cn^{1/2}}\big).

Following the same steps as in Proposition
C.3
, but this time using the more precise expansion
R^{(n)}(z)=I+\frac{R_{1}(z)}{n}+O\big(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\big)
in the expression for
\tilde{\mu}
, we see,

\displaystyle-\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}(z)\big(v_{\Sigma}(z)^{(n)}-\tilde{v}^{(n)}_{\Sigma}(z)\big)\big(\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big)^{-1}(z){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz

\displaystyle\qquad{}=-2\pi n\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\left(1+\frac{R_{1}(z)}{n}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)E(z)\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)&-\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\\
\Psi_{22}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)&-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\end{matrix}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}&0\\
0&1\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}\times E^{-1}(z)\left(1-\frac{R_{1}(z)}{n}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)\left[\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(z)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(z)-2\right)\right]{\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz,

and therefore,

\displaystyle-\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}(z)\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}(z)-\tilde{v}^{(n)}_{\Sigma}(z)\big)\big(\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big)^{-1}(z){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}+\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n+1}}\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}(z)\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}(z)-\tilde{v}^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}(z)\big)\big(\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}\big)^{-1}(z){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz

\displaystyle\qquad{}=-2\pi n\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\left(1+\frac{R_{1}(z)}{n}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)E(z)\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)&-\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\\
\Psi_{22}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)&-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}&0\\
0&1\end{matrix}\right)E^{-1}(z)\left(1-\frac{R_{1}(z)}{n}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)\times\left[\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(z)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(z)-2\right)\right]{\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}+2\pi(n+1)\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n+1}}\left(1+\frac{R_{1}(z)}{n+1}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)E(z)\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&\dfrac{1}{2\pi(n+1)}\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big((n+1)^{2}f(z)\big)&-\Psi_{12}^{2}\big((n+1)^{2}f(z)\big)\\
\Psi_{22}^{2}\big((n+1)^{2}f(z)\big)&-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big((n+1)^{2}f(z)\big)\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}\dfrac{1}{2\pi(n+1)}&0\\
0&1\end{matrix}\right)E^{-1}(z)\left(1-\frac{R_{1}(z)}{(n+1)}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}\times\left[\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(z)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(z)-2\right)\right]{\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz.

(C.11)

Now, let
y_{1}=n^{2}f(z)
and
y_{2}=(n+1)^{2}f(z)
, note that if
z\in[1,1+1/n]
then
0<y_{1},y_{2}<cn
. Let
f_{1}^{-1}
refer to the inverse of
f
within a neighborhood of 1. Then for
z\in[1,1+1/n]
, and some constant
a
,

\displaystyle{\rm d}z=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\big(f_{1}^{-1}\big)^{\prime}\left(\frac{y_{1}}{n^{2}}\right){\rm d}y_{1}=\frac{2{\rm d}y_{1}}{n^{2}}\left(1+\frac{ay_{1}}{n^{2}}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right),

\displaystyle{\rm d}z=\frac{1}{(n+1)^{2}}\big(f_{1}^{-1}\big)^{\prime}\left(\frac{y_{2}}{(n+1)^{2}}\right){\rm d}y_{2}=\frac{2{\rm d}y_{2}}{(n+1)^{2}}\left(1+\frac{ay_{2}}{(n+1)^{2}}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right).

Making the substitution
y_{1}=n^{2}f(z)
in the first integral of (
C.11
) and
y_{2}=(n+1)^{2}f(z)
in the second, we obtain

\displaystyle-\frac{4\pi}{n}\int_{0}^{n^{2}f\left(1+\frac{1}{n}\right)}\left(1+\frac{R_{1}\big(f_{1}^{-1}\big(\frac{y_{1}}{n^{2}}\big)\big)}{n}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)E\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y_{1}}{n^{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}\end{matrix}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}(y_{1})&-\Psi_{12}^{2}(y_{1})\\
\Psi_{22}^{2}(y_{1})&-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}(y_{1})\end{matrix}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}&0\\
0&1\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times E^{-1}\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y_{1}}{n^{2}}\right)\right)\left(1-\frac{R_{1}\big(f_{1}^{-1}\big(\frac{y_{1}}{n^{2}}\big)\big)}{n}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times\Bigg[\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y_{1}}{n^{2}}\right)\right)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y_{1}}{n^{2}}\right)\right)-2\right)\left(1+\frac{ay_{1}}{n^{2}}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)\Bigg]{\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006pty_{1}

\displaystyle\qquad{}+\frac{4\pi}{n+1}\int_{0}^{(n+1)^{2}f\left(1+\frac{1}{n+1}\right)}\!\left(1+\frac{R_{1}\big(f_{1}^{-1}\big(\frac{y_{2}}{(n+1)^{2}}\big)\big)}{n+1}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)E\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y_{2}}{(n+1)^{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&\dfrac{1}{2\pi(n+1)}\end{matrix}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}(y_{2})&-\Psi_{12}^{2}(y_{2})\\
\Psi_{22}^{2}(y_{2})&-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}(y_{2})\end{matrix}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}\frac{1}{2\pi(n+1)}&0\\
0&1\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times E^{-1}\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y_{2}}{(n+1)^{2}}\right)\right)\left(1-\frac{R_{1}\big(f_{1}^{-1}\big(\frac{y_{2}}{(n+1)^{2}}\big)\big)}{n+1}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times\Bigg[\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y_{2}}{(n+1)^{2}}\right)\right)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y_{2}}{(n+1)^{2}}\right)\right)-2\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times\left(1+\frac{ay_{2}}{(n+1)^{2}}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006pty_{2}.

(C.12)

Note that
(n+1)^{2}f\big(1+\frac{1}{n+1}\big)=O(n)
and
(n+1)^{2}f\big(1+\frac{1}{n+1}\big)-n^{2}f\big(1+\frac{1}{n}\big)=O(1)
. Therefore, since all of the terms in the above integrands are bounded for large
y
, and since
\Psi_{12}
,
\Psi_{22}
rapidly decay for large argument, we can replace the limits of the second integral in (
C.12
) with the limits of the first and pick up an error that is exponentially small in
n
. Next, recall that

\displaystyle f^{-1}(y)-1=2y+ay^{2}+O\big(y^{3}\big)

and
y
is in the region
\big|f^{-1}\big(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\big)-1\big|\leqslant\frac{1}{n}
, with

\displaystyle\frac{f^{-1}\big(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\big)}{f^{-1}\big(\frac{y}{(n+1)^{2}}\big)}=\frac{2\frac{y}{n^{2}}\big(1+O\big(\frac{1}{n}\big)\big)}{2\frac{y}{(n+1)^{2}}\big(1+O\big(\frac{1}{n}\big)\big)}=1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

So, by Proposition
2.5
, we see that

\displaystyle\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\right)\right)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\right)\right)-\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{(n+1)^{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{(n+1)^{2}}\right)\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{n\log^{3}n}\right).

Next, using the analyticity of
E
,
R_{1}
,

\displaystyle E\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\right)\right)-E\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{(n+1)^{2}}\right)\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)

and

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{R_{1}\big(f_{1}^{-1}\big(\frac{y_{2}}{n^{2}}\big)\big)}{n}\right)-\left(1+\frac{R_{1}\big(f_{1}^{-1}\big(\frac{y_{2}}{(n+1)^{2}}\big)\big)}{n+1}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right),

and clearly

\displaystyle\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}\end{matrix}\right)-\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&\dfrac{1}{2\pi(n+1)}\end{matrix}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right).

Thus, subtracting (
C.12
) term by term, (
C.12
) is

\displaystyle\left(-\frac{4\pi}{n}+\frac{4\pi}{n+1}\right)\int_{0}^{n^{2}f\left(1+\frac{1}{n}\right)}\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)E\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}\end{matrix}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}(y)&-\Psi_{12}^{2}(y)\\
\Psi_{22}^{2}(y)&-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}(y)\end{matrix}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}&0\\
0&1\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times E^{-1}\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\right)\right)\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times\left[\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\right)\right)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\right)\right)-2\right)\right]{\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006pty

\displaystyle\qquad{}+\frac{4\pi}{n}O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\left\|\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}(y)&-\Psi_{12}^{2}(y)\\
\Psi_{22}^{2}(y)&-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}(y)\end{matrix}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad{}\times\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\right)\right)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\right)\right)-2\right)\right\|_{L^{1}(0,n^{2}f(1+\frac{1}{n}))}

\displaystyle\qquad{}+\frac{4\pi}{n}O\left(\frac{1}{n\log^{3}n}\right)\left\|\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{11}\Psi_{22}(y)&-\Psi_{12}^{2}(y)\\
\Psi_{22}^{2}(y)&-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}(y)\end{matrix}\right)\right\|_{L^{1}(0,n^{2}f(1+1/n))}.

(C.13)

The function
\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}-2
is bounded in compact sets and
\Psi_{12}
,
\Psi_{22}
are both
L^{2}(0,\infty)
, therefore the
L^{1}
norms are both bounded uniformly of
n
. Therefore (
C.13
) is

\displaystyle-\frac{4\pi}{n(n+1)}\int_{0}^{n^{2}f\left(1+\frac{1}{n}\right)}\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)E\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}\end{matrix}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{11}\Psi_{22}(y)&-\Psi_{12}^{2}(y)\\
\Psi_{22}^{2}(y)&-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}(y)\end{matrix}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}\frac{1}{2\pi n}&0\\
0&1\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times E^{-1}\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\right)\right)\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\left[\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\right)\right)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}\left(f_{1}^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{n^{2}}\right)\right)-2\right)\right]{\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006pty+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\right).

(C.14)

Inverting the change of variables
y=n^{2}f(z)
, (
C.14
) becomes

\displaystyle-\frac{2\pi n}{n+1}\int_{1}^{1+\frac{1}{n}}\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)E(z)\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)&-\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\\
\Psi_{22}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)&-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(z)\big)\end{matrix}\right)\left(\begin{matrix}\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}&0\\
0&1\end{matrix}\right)E^{-1}(z)\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times\left[\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w_{+}}(z)+\frac{F^{2}}{w_{-}}(z)-2\right)\right]{\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\right).

(C.15)

However, the integral here is exactly
\frac{1}{n+1}
times the integral in (
C.7
), and therefore (
C.15
) evaluates to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n+1}\left(\frac{3}{16n\log^{2}n}\left(\begin{matrix}1&-i\\
-i&-1\end{matrix}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n\log^{3}n}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{3}{16n^{2}\log^{2}n}\left(\begin{matrix}1&-i\\
-i&-1\end{matrix}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\right),

(C.16)

which is the right-hand side of (
C.10
).

To conclude the proof we need to show the contribution from the left endpoint,
I_{2}
, is of lower order. Following the same steps as those leading up to (
C.11
), we have

\displaystyle I_{2}=-2\pi n\int_{(\Sigma\backslash[-1,1])\cap U_{1/n}(-1)}\sigma_{3}\left(1+\frac{R_{1}(-z)}{n}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)E(-z)\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_2 =}{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)&\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)\\
-\Psi_{22}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)&\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_2 =}{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}\dfrac{1}{2\pi n}&0\\
0&1\end{matrix}\right)E^{-1}(-z)\left(1-\frac{R_{1}(-z)}{n}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)\sigma_{3}\left[\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w}(z)-1\right)\right]{\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_2 =}{}+2\pi(n+1)\int_{(\Sigma\backslash[-1,1])\cap U_{1/n}(-1)}\!\sigma_{3}\left(1+\frac{R_{1}(-z)}{n+1}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)E(-z)\left(\begin{matrix}1&0\\
0&\dfrac{1}{2\pi(n+1)}\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_2 =}{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big((n+1)^{2}f(-z)\big)&\Psi_{12}^{2}\big((n+1)^{2}f(-z)\big)\\
-\Psi_{22}^{2}\big((n+1)^{2}f(-z)\big)&\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big((n+1)^{2}f(-z)\big)\end{matrix}\right)

\displaystyle\hphantom{I_2 =}{}\times\left(\begin{matrix}\dfrac{1}{2\pi(n+1)}\!&0\\
0&1\end{matrix}\right)E^{-1}(-z)\left(1-\frac{R_{1}(-z)}{(n+1)}+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)\sigma_{3}\left[\left(\frac{F^{2}}{w}(z)-1\right)\right]{\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz.\!\!\!\!

(C.17)

Subtracting (
C.17
) term by term,

\displaystyle|I_{2}|\leqslant c\int_{(\Sigma\backslash[-1,1])\cap U_{1/n}(-1)}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)&\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)\\
-\Psi_{22}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)&\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)\end{matrix}\right)\right|\left|\frac{F^{2}}{w}(z)-1\right||{\rm d}z|

\displaystyle\hphantom{|I_2 |\leqslant}{}+cn\int_{(\Sigma\backslash[-1,1])\cap U_{1/n}(-1)}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big((n+1)^{2}f(-z)\big)&\Psi_{12}^{2}\big((n+1)^{2}f(-z)\big)\\
-\Psi_{22}^{2}\big((n+1)^{2}f(-z)\big)&\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big((n+1)^{2}f(-z)\big)\end{matrix}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hphantom{|I_{2}|\leqslant}{}-\left(\begin{matrix}-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)&\Psi_{12}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)\\
-\Psi_{22}^{2}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)&\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\big(n^{2}f(-z)\big)\end{matrix}\right)\right|\left|\frac{F^{2}}{w}(z)-1\right||{\rm d}z|.

(C.18)

Recall from (
B.29
) that for
|z+1|\leqslant\frac{1}{n}
,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{F^{2}}{w}(z)-1\right|=O\big(n^{-1/2}\big).

(C.19)

Making the change of variables
y=n^{2}f(-z)
and using (
C.19
), (
C.18
) becomes

\displaystyle cn^{-5/2}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leqslant|y|\leqslant O(n)\\
\arg y=\pm 2\pi/3\end{subarray}}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}(y)&\Psi_{12}^{2}(y)\\
-\Psi_{22}^{2}(y)&\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}(y)\end{matrix}\right)\right||{\rm d}y|

\displaystyle\qquad{}+cn^{-3/2}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leqslant|y|\leqslant O(n)\\
\arg y=\pm 2\pi/3\end{subarray}}\left|\left(\begin{matrix}-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\left(\dfrac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y\right)&\Psi_{12}^{2}\left(\dfrac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y\right)\\
-\Psi_{22}^{2}\left(\dfrac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y\right)&\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\left(\dfrac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y\right)\end{matrix}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad{}-\left(\begin{matrix}-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}(y)&\Psi_{12}^{2}(y)\\
-\Psi_{22}^{2}(y)&\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}(y)\end{matrix}\right)\right||{\rm d}y|

\displaystyle\quad{}\leqslant c_{1}n^{-5/2}+c_{2}n^{-5/2}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leqslant|y|\leqslant O(n)\\
\arg y=\pm 2\pi/3\end{subarray}}\left|y\sup_{\left(y,\frac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y\right)}\frac{{\rm d}}{{\rm d}x}\left(\begin{matrix}-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\left(x\right)&\Psi_{12}^{2}(x)\\
-\Psi_{22}^{2}(x)&\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}(x)\end{matrix}\right)\right||{\rm d}y|.

Recall that
\Psi_{2}(y)=O(\log y)
and
\Psi_{2}^{\prime}(y)=O\big(\frac{1}{y}\big)
as
y\to 0
, and
\Psi_{2}(y),\Psi_{2}^{\prime}(y)=O\big(e^{-cy^{1/2}}\big)
as
y\to\infty
, see (
B.14
). Therefore

\displaystyle\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leqslant|y|<\infty\\
\arg y=\pm 2\pi/3\end{subarray}}\left|y\sup_{\left(y,\frac{(n+1)^{2}}{n^{2}}y\right)}\frac{{\rm d}}{{\rm d}x}\left(\begin{matrix}-\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}\left(x\right)&\Psi_{12}^{2}(x)\\
-\Psi_{22}^{2}(x)&\Psi_{12}\Psi_{22}(x)\end{matrix}\right)\right||{\rm d}y|=O(1),

and so

\displaystyle|I_{2}|=O\big(n^{-5/2}\big),

which, together with (
C.16
) implies

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma}\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}(z)\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n)}(z)-\tilde{v}^{(n)}_{\Sigma}(z)\big)\big(\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}\big)^{-1}(z){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz

\displaystyle\qquad\quad{}-\int_{\Sigma}\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}(z)\big(v_{\Sigma}^{(n+1)}(z)-\tilde{v}^{(n+1)}_{\Sigma}(z)\big)\big(\tilde{\mu}^{(n+1)}\big)^{-1}(z){\rm d}\hskip-1.00006pt\bar{}\hskip 1.00006ptz

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{3}{16n^{2}\log^{2}n}\left(\begin{matrix}1&-i\\
-i&-1\end{matrix}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\log^{3}n}\right)

completing the proof of Proposition
C.4
.
∎
